## Document 016: 20260827-台新投顧複委託報告-NVDA FY2Q27財報Call memo

請參考末頁之免責宣言說明                                                                           1

NVIDIA Corp. (NVDA-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — FY2Q27 NVDA 財報亮眼，初步給予 FY28 70% 強勁成長指引

個股基本資料： 
NVIDIA Corp. (NVDA-US) 
日期 2026/08/26 
收盤價 $209.66 
股價表現 
三個月報酬(%) -2.42 
六個月報酬(%) 13.40 
一年報酬率(%) 15.34 
交易資料 
市值(十億) $5,155.81 
30 日平均成交量 118,052,066 
52 週高低 $164.07- $236.54 
市場評等 
市場預估 12M 目標價 $317.33 
買進 79 
持有 2 
賣出 1 
資料來源：Bloomberg、FactSet 
 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
NVDA FY2Q27 營收獲利優預期。財報公布時，股價盤後一度因 Q3 毛利率
受記憶體漲價承壓而走低，惟隨法說會揭露亞馬遜擴大採購，且提前給出  
FY2028 全年營收年增～70% 的強勁指引（顯著優於市場預期的 45%）後，
股價翻揚至上漲 4%。公司強調 70% 增速仍受限於產能（真實需求增長逾  
100%），一掃先前 AI 資本支出放緩疑慮。整體而言，短線雜音（毛利率壓
抑、循環融資疑慮）不改 Agentic AI 算力剛性需求的長線邏輯，隨著 Vera 
Rubin 於 8 月全面量產、Vera CPU（切入 2,000 億美元 TAM）與網路業務
持續擴大非 GPU 營收來源，加上聯手頂級機構籌集逾 5,000 億美元資本解
鎖生態系融資限制，算力霸主地位穩固，長線獲利動能可望延續。  
公司財報重點數據： 
NVDA FY2Q27 營收 962.2 億美元，QoQ+18%、YoY+106%，連續四個季
度加速成長，優於市場預期的 920 億美元。Non-GAAP GM 為 75 %，符合
預期。Non-GAAP EPS 達 2.22 美元，優於市場預期。自由現金流達 213.4 
億美元，較上季明顯下滑，主因應收帳款及存貨等營運資本占用增加。季度
股利維持每股 0.25 美元，Q2 以庫藏股與股利合計返還股東 260 億美元，
剩餘庫藏股額度 990 億美元。 
業務部門表現： 
資料中心：營收 890 億美元，QoQ+18%、YoY+117%，成長動能加速且
優於市場預期 。細分業務： 超大型雲端（ Hyperscale）營收 487 億美元
（QoQ+13%），優於市場預期，受惠 Blackwell Ultra 出貨持續強勁，並宣
布擴大與 AWS 合作，追加部署 200 萬顆 GPU；ACIE（涵蓋 AI 雲端、工
業、企業） 營收為  403 億美元（ QoQ+25%、YoY+138%），略低於預
期，成長動能 來自新雲端服務商（ Neo cloud ）產能擴充 。主權 AI 營收
QoQ+35%、YoY 逾 3 倍。 
邊緣運算：營收 72 億美元，QoQ+13%、YoY+27.5%，優於預期，主要由
受惠 Blackwell 工作站需求帶動，有效抵銷了消費性 PC 市場因記憶體及系
統漲價帶來的疲軟。 
公司展望： 
(1). 公 司 預 估FY3Q27 營收 1,080 億美元 (±2%) ， 優 於 市 場 預 期;GM 
74%(±50 bps) ，低於市場預期，主因記憶體零組件價格漲幅超出預期 ，預
計毛利率將於 Q4 打底後、隨 FY1Q28 執行漲價後逐步回升至 72~73%。 
(2). FY2027 全年  Non-GAAP OpEx 年增率上修至 low 50s ，稅率維持  
複委託報告 August  27, 2026

### Page 2

2
16%~18%。(3). Vera Rubin 8 月量產，預計佔 Q3 資料中心營收約 20%，
每百萬瓦吞吐量提升 30 倍。(4).初步預估 FY2028 全年營收成長約 70%（受
限於供應鏈產能 ，未受限需求高達  100%+），產能瓶頸預計至少持續至
FY2028 年底。(5). 獨立式 Vera CPU 正式量產，預計 FY2028 CPU 營收預
期翻倍（前 12 個月 Grace CPU 營收已破 50 億美元），確立其成為全球領
先伺服器 CPU 供應商。(6). 中國市場：中國資料中心運算(H200)營收比重
<1%，未來財測未納入任何中國資料中心運算營收。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
(1). 70%指引 vs. 100% 無限制需求的缺口：公司表示 70%為供給受限下可
確信兌現的數字，實際需求成長已達 100%，缺口主因在土地／電力／廠房
建置（Shell）等基礎建設籌建期偏長，而非單純晶片產能問題。(2). 推論市
占與客製化晶片防線： 強調 NVIDIA 為唯一橫跨全生命週期的全端平台，

Grok 3 LPX機架級系統已量產，定位為補強性產品，絕大多數資料中心仍以
Vera Rubin 平台為主。(3). 算力金融與資本：聯手 Apollo、BlackRock 等
六大機構籌集  >$500B 第三方資本，解決前沿 AI 實驗室融資限制；推行
Neo Cloud 營收分潤機制，提供最低營收擔保以獲取租金分成，開闢高複利
衍生收入。(4). Agentic AI 算力：推理調用需多消耗 15~100 倍算力，驅動
二次加速。平台完全兼容閉源（OpenAI）與開源模型（DeepSeek）。
公司簡介、競爭對手與公司自身價值：
NVIDIA 已成功由晶片巨頭轉型為全球  AI 算力工廠與基礎設施的生態系 霸
主。其護城河建立在  GPU、Vera CPU 、高階網路（ NVLink/Spectrum-
X）、 CUDA 軟體系與  Grok LPU 的「極端共同設計」（ Extreme Co -
design）。每吉瓦（GW）資料中心帶給 NVDA 的營收潛力已從 Hopper 時

代的 180 億美元大幅提升至 Vera Rubin 的 400 億美元。公司透過第三方資
本平台與 Neo Cloud 營收分潤，建立起客製化晶片（ASIC/ XPU）競爭對手
無法複製的商業模式與資金生態網，持續鞏固其在 Agentic AI 時代無可動搖
的平台優勢。

順德 (2351 TT) 
均熱片展望越趨明確

永豐觀點 
毛利率表現優於預期；AI 出貨進度超前；均熱片為潛在新動能。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：3Q26 受惠銅利差有利，AI 比重提升與急單附加費，預估毛利率持續向
上，AI 相關產品營收逐漸放大 ， 預期3Q26 比重即可佔到電子產品裡面的比重 10%
以上，2027 年 AI 營收比重可在 10%的基礎上往上墊，樂觀的話評估單月可望挑
戰 20%的營收比重，此外均熱片的潛在產值貢獻龐大，有利評價表現，預估2026
年/2027 年稅後 EPS 6.99 元/8.53 元，維持買進的評等，目標價由 195 元上修至
255 元(約當 2027 年 EPS*PER30X)。

ESG 評析 
順德企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

主要功率元件大廠皆為客戶 ：順德成立於 1967 年 ， 以辦公文具業務起家 ； 於1983
年成立電子事業部門，生產半導體導線架，於 2010 年引進日本三菱伸銅
(mitsubishi-shindoh)成為策略夥伴，取得半導體特殊材料之製程，提供相關技術
之授權與支援 。 主要客戶群則包括Infineon 、STM、ON SEMI、Vishay、Nexperia
等 IDM(整合元件廠)。 
2Q26 毛利率優於預期：2Q26 毛利率達 20.72%，表現優於預期主因，雖然銅價
利差貢獻相較 1Q26 降低很多，但在漲價效應、良率效率稼動率提高，降本(修繕
/電費/化學品)的努力之下，毛利率仍較 1Q26 提升，2Q26 費用達 3.2 億元，其中
包含了一次性的 5000 萬費用，稅後 EPS 1.54 元(原先預估 1.34 元)，表現優於預
期。 
評估 3Q26 毛利率持續向上：展望 3Q26 AI 相關需求強勁，AI 相關產品的營收比
重在 7 月已顯著上升，預期 3Q26 電子產品裡，AI 相關營收比重有望挑戰雙位數
以上的營收佔比，車用市場亦明顯復甦，因此3Q26 將有急單的額外附加費貢獻，
另外銅價利差也有利，而 AI 相關則屬於高毛率的產品線，研究部預估 3Q26 營收
39.8 億元(+11.7%QoQ，+56%YoY)，毛利率 22.47%，稅後 EPS 2.22 元。 
AI 相關需求持續強勁：AI 相關的在手項目已由原本的 40 個提升至 60 個品項；相
關產品包括： PSU、BBU、主板正反面導線架、 800V HVDC 、Power Stage 與
iQFN(低伏特) 等應用，AI 導線架產品以 CLIP 與 To 為大宗(主要用於需要高散熱
的產品)，CLIP 毛利率優於平均，AI 相關佔電子營收的比重從去年的 1% 大幅成
長至 1H26 的 6%，預期在 3Q26 有機會達到雙位數佔比，倘若 60 個品項全部
發酵，評估單月有望佔整體營收比重 20%的水準。 
跨入均熱片領域 ， 潛在產值貢獻龐大 ：公司擁有沖壓 、 金屬加工與電鍍等製程能力 ，
順勢投入開發均熱片產品， 均熱片生產主要有沖壓或是鍛造的方式， 沖壓方式以消
費性產品為主， 順德導線架共平面度可達 20-30 微米 ， 而均熱片客戶僅需 70-80 
微米即可，順德已是四家客戶(包含 IC 設計公司與封測廠)的合格供應商，評估潛
在需求達單月 2,000 萬片；為滿足客戶強勁需求，順德將有積極的擴產計畫，未
來不排除有籌資需求，初步預計在半年內將均熱片產能先擴充至單月  100 萬到 
200 萬片，均熱片市場應用包括：CPU/GPU 均熱片、光通訊均熱片，以及車用與
ASIC 等其他應用，目前發展重心主要在光通訊以及車用、ASIC 等，雖然 2026 年

佳世達 (2352 TT)  Trading Buy

考量「全面 AI 化」成效、旺季效應，評等為 Trading Buy 。 
投資建議 ：由於 2H 為螢幕、投影機等 IT 產品旺季，  BSG 、醫
療、NCG 等高附加價值事業部門營收成長性仍佳， 2H26 營收展
望優於 1H26 ，加上考量「全面 AI 化」策略成效逐漸顯現，群
益對佳世達的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 5.94 億元， QoQ+87.05% ：受惠於 BSG、醫療相
關營收成長， 2Q26 營收 527.30 億元， QoQ+2.31% ，其中， BSG
營收 113 億元， QoQ+11.88% ，已連續 2 季創新高；醫療相關營
收 83 億元， QoQ+10.67% 。在產品結構轉佳下，毛利率提高至
17.27% ，但因螢幕等 IT 產品銷售狀況轉差、研發費用增加，
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ-5.90% ，惟受惠於友達等策略性轉投資的評
價利益，稅後淨利增加至 5.94 億元， QoQ+87.05% 。  
若就 1H26 各主要事業部門營運狀況而言，首先， BSG 營收 214
億元， YoY+21.59% ， 1Q26 、 2Q26 連續 2 季創新高，動能來自
AI 服務、邊緣 AI、自動化相關需求增溫；其次， NCG 營收 105
億元， YoY+2.94% ，且虧損數字已低於 1H25 ，營運體質逐漸改
善；醫療相關營收 1H26 穩定增加至 158 億元，但營業利益低於
1H25 ，原因在於明基醫院二期相關營收仍未達經濟規模。  
3Q26 營收展望優於 2Q26 ：佳世達現階段營運策略以「全面 AI
化」為主軸 ， 計畫將硬體產品結合 AI 軟體 、AI 應用 。 展望2H26，
在各主要事業部中，營收成長性乃以 BSG 、醫療、 NCG 較佳，
其中，預估以 BSG 的營收 YoY 最高，動能來自 AIoT 衍生商
機、 server 等 AI 基礎建設相關訂單；醫療相關營收預估將穩定
成長，原因在於明基醫院二期院區住房率持續提高；至於 NCG
的營收成長性則將來自資料中心、矽光子相關訂單。  
預估 2H26 營收表現將優於 1H26 ，原因在於螢幕、投影機等 IT
產品進入旺季，且 3Q26 營收將高於 2Q26 ，4Q26 營收雖預估將
低於 3Q26 ，但包括液冷 server 等 AI 基礎建設相關新產品將於
4Q26 開始出貨，將為長線營收潛在動能。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 24 日

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，佳世達將產品生命週期思
維結合於設計流程，且於早期設計階段即納入綠色設計目標，並於各設計階
段檢核，確認產品除符合客戶、銷售國法規外，並提升產品的能源效率減少
資源的耗用，持續推動「綠色設計 555( 節能 5%、減量 5%、減碳 5%) 」目
標，以降低環境負荷同時提升產品的效能，並提供客戶更優質的產品與建立
產品的永續價值。  
再就產品碳足跡減量、能效標章而言，首先，佳世達建立碳管理平台，產品
於量產後即可產出 「搖籃到門口」 (Cradle-to-Gate 或稱 Business-to-Business, 
B2B) 之自我宣告產品碳足跡報告 (Carbon Footprint of Product Report, 或稱
CFP Report) ，且 2012 年起 100% 主要機種皆具有碳足跡報告。其次，佳世
達要求產品於研發初期即具備綠色設計的思維，評估設計的產品 /零件於生
命週期各階段中可能對環境的衝擊與風險，並於設計源頭做好管理，且著重
設計研發階段以減量、有害物質管理、節能、回收等四大綠色設計方向。  
受惠於策略性轉投資的評價利益 2Q26 稅後淨利 5.94 億元， QoQ+87.05% ：
佳世達以生產螢幕、投影機等 IT 產品起家，除承接代工訂單外，亦以 BenQ
自有品牌銷售，然而，為因應產品毛利率走低的產業困境，佳世達不但加強
耕耘高階市場 (IT_HVA ，高附加價值 IT 產品 )，更跨足網通 (NCG) 、智慧方
案 (BSG) 、醫療等高附加價值領域，目前合計營收比重達 40~55% 。其中，網
通產品的營收多數來自明泰 (3380 TT) 及其轉投資的仲琦 (2419 TT) 、互動
(6486 TT) ，現階段重要經營方向之一為整合明泰、仲琦的客源，並提高仲琦
Hitron 自有品牌的產品齊全度及銷售規模。  
智慧方案產品則涵蓋企業 IT( 資訊技術 )智慧化 (包括高速網路運算、資安 /網
安、雲地整合、數位轉型等 )、企業 OT( 營運技術 )智慧化 (包括綠能、自動化、
工業電腦等 )、 OMO( 虛實整合 )服務等 3 大垂直市場，並主要由邁達特 (6112 
TT) 、友通 (2397 TT) 、拍檔 (3097 TT) 分別負責，目前 BSG 的重要營運目標
為整合集團內部的研發、行銷資源，以拉高智慧方案產品的毛利率。  
醫療領域的營收來源主要包括醫療服務 (南京、蘇州明基醫院 )、設備耗材 (手
術照明、牙科耗材、血液透析耗材等 )，後續營運重心除評估新增醫院外，
擴大血液透析耗材等發展空間較大的設備耗材產品線、諾貝兒 (6844 TT) 旗
下的「丁丁藥局」更為後續重要成長動能，尤其，設備耗材的毛利率高於醫
療服務，對提升佳世達獲利的助益將更勝營收。  
2Q26 營收 527.30 億元， QoQ+2.31% ，動能主要來自 BSG、醫療，其中， BSG
營收 113 億元， QoQ+11.88% ，已連續 2 季創新高；醫療相關營收 83 億元，
QoQ+10.67% 。在產品結構轉佳下，毛利率提高至 17.27%(1Q26 為 16.67%) ，
但因螢幕等 IT 產品銷售狀況轉差、研發費用增加， 2Q26 營業利益 QoQ-
5.90% ，惟受惠於友達等策略性轉投資的評價利益，稅後淨利增加至 5.94 億
元， QoQ+87.05% 。  
若就 1H26 各主要事業部門營運狀況而言，首先， BSG 營收 214 億元，
YoY+21.59% ， 1Q26 、 2Q26 連續 2 季創新高，動能來自 AI 服務、邊緣 AI、
自動化相關需求增溫；其次， NCG 營收 105 億元， YoY+2.94% ，且虧損數
字已低於 1H25，營運體質逐漸改善；醫療相關營收 1H26 穩定增加至 158 億

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 24 日

3 群益投顧 
元，但營業利益低於 1H25 ，原因在於明基醫院二期院區相關營收仍未達經
濟規模。  
因 IT 產品旺季，以及 BSG 、醫療、 NCG 營收持續成長， 3Q26 展望優於
2Q26 ：  
佳世達現階段營運策略以 「全面 AI 化」 為主軸，計畫將硬體產品結合 AI 軟
體、 AI 應用。展望 2H26，在各主要事業部中，營收成長性乃以 BSG、醫療、
NCG 較佳，其中，預估以 BSG 的營收 YoY 最高，動能來自 AIoT 衍生商機、
server 等 AI 基礎建設相關訂單；醫療相關營收預估將穩定成長，原因在於
明基醫院二期院區住房率持續提高；至於 NCG 的營收成長性則將來自資料
中心、矽光子相關訂單。  
觀察目前接單狀況，預估 2H26 營收表現將優於 1H26 ，原因在於螢幕、投
影機等 IT 產品進入旺季，且 3Q26 營收將高於 2Q26 ， 4Q26 營收雖預估將
低於 3Q26 ，但包括液冷 server 、 1.6T 液冷資料中心 switch 、雙相液冷散熱
解決方案等 AI 基礎建設相關新產品將於 4Q26 開始出貨，將為長線營收潛
在動能。  
考量「全面 AI 化」轉型成效漸顯現、 IT 產品旺季效應，群益對佳世達的投
資建議為 Trading Buy ：  
由於 2H 為螢幕、投影機等 IT 產品旺季，  BSG 、醫療、 NCG 等高附加價值
事業部門營收成長性仍佳， 2H26 營收展望優於 1H26 ，加上考量「全面 AI
化」策略成效逐漸顯現，群益對佳世達的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

4
Chroma ATE
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,500 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG. Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical
range as its growth outlook is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its revenue are now AI-
related, with strong growth across product segments including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and well positioned to capitalized on the global AI expansion.

Risks
Downside risks to our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth expectation due to changes in capex outlook or production
bottleneck; 2) fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate improves; 3) disappointing metrology
shipment due to changes at ramp-up schedule; 4) testing process change leading to loss in market share; 5) FX risks (50-60%
of Chroma’s sales are in US$, 10-20% in Rmb).
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

We think Nvidia’s recent guidance for 70% revenue growth in
CY27 should be positive for members of Taiwan's supply chain
with higher related revenue exposure.
Please see Joe Moore’s take on Nvidia’s recent results:  NVIDIA very positive 2027

commentary (27 Aug 2026). Among other highlights, 70%+ CY27 revenue growth
guidance is a remarkable figure, and to the extent possible we would expect Nvidia
to continue to knock down barriers to higher growth. Management framed the
demand as "much greater" than that 70% level. That echoes our  previous 2027
CoWoS forecast in  Asia-Pacific Technology: AI Supply Chain: Preliminary 2027 TSMC
CoWoS Allocation (23 Jun 2026): Nvidia uses TSMC’s CoWoS-L as the single source

for its AI GPU products (e.g., Blackwell and Rubin). Its 2027 CoWoS-L consumption
could reach ~910k, up ~40% Y/Y. Strong CoWoS-R bookings by Nvidia also suggest
very strong Vera CPU shipments (almost doubling). Factoring in the chip price hike
starting in 1Q next year, 70%+ revenue growth looks consistent with TSMC CoWoS
implied chip shipments.
Is that a strong catalyst for Nvidia’s supply chain in Taiwan? We think yes: We
cite the strong growth outlook, ongoing tightness, and cheap valuation:

• We still think TSMC’s wafer front-end (3nm) and ABF substrate supply
remain major constraints, as Nvidia goes through HBM spec optimization
(see Asia-Pacific Technology: AI Supply Chain: GPU Architecture Optimization
Given Memory Shortage and Supernode Challenge (10 Aug 2026) ). We
expect TSMC to succeed with a wafer price hike  in 2027 – we view a 5%-10%
hike as likely.
• KYEC’s 3Q26 revenue suffered from wafer constraints. With Rubin ramping
on schedule, we expect strong sequential growth in 4Q26 for KYEC’s GPU

and CPU revenue.
•  See Exhibit 2  for Nvidia revenue exposure across semiconductor and tech
hardware. Most stocks’ 2027e PEG still screens below 0.7 on average.

Aligning Blackwell and Rubin chip shipments with HGX and rack shipments
We analyzed Blackwell chip shipments versus rack shipments in our  last  June AI tracker.
We believe TSMC's Blackwell chip output, based on CoWoS-L capacity, aligns more closely

with underlying demand as reflected in AI capex trends.
We now incorporate HGX systems (8 GPUs per server) into our chip consumption model
and assume that nine HGX servers are equivalent to one NVL72 rack. We forecast 5.4mn
Blackwell units in 2026, with chip supply sufficient to meet Grace Blackwell NVL72
demand by 2H26.
Our latest checks also suggest shipments of nearly 7mn Rubin and Rubin Ultra units in
2027, while total Rubin NVL72 rack shipments could reach 90k units in 2027 (Exhibit 9 ).

All Blackwell chip "inventory" has proven to be supply-chain buffer stock and should be
fully absorbed by 2026. As a result, we see little reason for concern over inventory levels.
We expect Rubin to follow a similar pattern. Rubin should begin ramping up in 3Q26, with
rack shipments starting in 4Q26.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

2Q26 GP in line: Compal’s 2Q26 revenue was up 32% YoY / 18% QoQ to NT$238bn, 
or 10% / 8% higher than our estimate / Bloomberg consensus, driven by strong AI 
server and PC consumption pull in. 2Q26 GM is lower than our estimate and 
consensus, reflecting the growing AI server business and rising raw material costs. GP 
increased by 4% QoQ, or 2% / 4% behind our / consensus, largely in line. Opex ratio 
of 3.4% is better than our estimate and consensus, reflecting the operational 
efficiency improvement, along with the higher than expected non-op income, 
leading 2Q26 net income up 549% YoY / 59% QoQ to NT$3bn, or 53% / 40% higher 
than our estimate / Bloomberg consensus.

### Page 2

Earnings revision: Factoring in Compal’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 results, we raise our 
2026-28E net income by 6% / 7% / 11%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex 
ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 6% / 8% / 10%, mainly on higher revenues 
from AI server business, supported by Compal’s client base expansion, global capacity 
expansion, and migration towards system level manufacturing. 2026-28E GMs are 
revised down, considering the rising AI server contribution. Our 2026-28E opex ratios 
are revised down to reflect Compal’s improving operational efficiency as revenue scales 
up.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12-month TP based on near-term P/E. Our 2027E 
target P/E is updated to 13.1x (vs. 13.6x previously), which is derived from the refreshed 
peers’ correlation of forward trading P/E and EPS growth, mainly on sector de-rating 
amid the rising component cost. With our updated earnings estimates and target 
multiple, our 12-month TP is raised to NT$41.3 (vs. NT$39.5 previously). Our target P/E 
is still around the company’s historical avg. forward trading P/E, considering the rising 
memory cost weighing on consumer electronics end demand, which still accounts for the 
majority of Compal’s total revenue. Maintain Neutral.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$41.3. Our target 
price is based on 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 27 Aug 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Unimicron

Limited clarity on the investigation; no 
material impact so far

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 1,400 TWD | Price: 1,110 TWD

Breaking News 
• According to the company’s announcement, Unimicron is being investigated by 
Taoyuan Investigation Bureau. Our supply chain checks and conversation with the 
company suggest this is on a matter relating to the labeling of products' origin. 
• Our supply chain checks and conversation with the company suggest that this does 
not appear to be related to accounting fraud or insider trading, as has been reported 
in some news items in the press. 
• Based on the limited information we have currently as described above, we do not see 
a major impact on the company's business/operation at this stage.  However, we do 
anticipate that uncertainty is likely to continue until there is further clarification.

Unimicron under investigation; share price dropped 
According to the company’s announcement, Taoyuan Investigation Bureau visited the 
company today to conduct a search/investigation, primarily to examine matters relating 
to “Offenses Against Agriculture, Industry, and Commerce”. The share price of 
Unimicron went down 5.9% today, vs 4.3% and 1.6% falls in the other two major 
substrate names NYPCB (Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board) and Kinsus.  
More granularities on the exact matter 
Our checks with the supply chain and with the company suggest this matter could be 
related to potential uncertainties associated with the labeling of products’ origin. 
Further, it appears it is neither related to accounting fraud nor insider trading, as has 
been reported in some news articles.  
Not seeing major impact on the business so far 
Currently we believe that in this case, based on the  information we have at this stage, 
there would only be a minor impact (if any) to the company’s product shipments, 
echoing the company’s statement of “no material impact on financial condition or 
business operation”. For other substrate names, we haven’t heard relevant repercussions 
so far.

Price objective basis & risk 
Unimicron (UMCRF) 
Our PO of NT$1,400 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E, which is roughly 1x standard 
deviation above the historical average of 20x since 2017. In our view, the multiple is 
appropriate as we expect Unimicron earnings to be supported by its high-end technology 
offerings, where end market demand seems more resilient.

Upside risks are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) greater PC and regular server demand, and (3) 
lower competition pressure.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) lower ASP content increase 
at AI server, and (3) bigger drop in the demand of consumer electronic products.

•MediaTek becomes a much more credible AI ASIC contender . MediaTek 
already expects its first major AI accelerator ASIC to enter production in 
4Q26, has guided to >$2bn of data-center revenue in 2026, estimates a

target share to 15–20%.   The NVIDIA relationship therefore potentially 
expands MediaTek’s opportunity beyond its existing hyperscaler programs: 
NVLink Fusion can become a customer-acquisition platform for future 
second customer's ASIC wins as NVLink Fusion changes the competitive 
proposition: instead of MediaTek selling customers only an independently 
designed ASIC, it can potentially offer a custom XPU already engineered to 
coexist with NVIDIA GPUs, CPUs, networking and rack-scale NVLink 
infrastructure. According to NVIDIA, the cooperation will be deepening 
including : NVIDIA NVLink Fusion chiplet, connecting XPUs to the NVIDIA 
NVLink scale-up fabric using NVIDIA photonics or electrical 
interconnects;  NVIDIA NVLink-C2C, providing high-bandwidth, energy-
efficient connectivity between XPUs, NVIDIA Rosa CPUs and other 
compatible processors. NVIDIA NVHBM, integrating customized memory 
capabilities to increase bandwidth and energy efficiency while dedicating 
more silicon area to compute.

collaborate across multiple generations of both RTX Spark and DGX Spark products.   MediaTek therefore gains three
separate AI growth vectors: (1) hyperscaler custom AI ASICs, (2) NVIDIA-based AI PCs/local AI systems, and (3)
automotive/physical AI. Strategically, this is important because MediaTek can reuse the same core capabilities—

across all three markets. The result should be a progressively higher mix of large, advanced-node chips with much
greater dollar content than traditional smartphone SoCs, reducing MediaTek’s dependence on handset cycles.
•We view today’s announcement as structurally positive for MediaTek. The US$3.5bn investment validates the
partnership financially, it potentially moves MediaTek from being a supplier that happens to have a few large AI ASIC
wins toward becoming one of the principal custom-silicon design platforms inside the broader NVIDIA AI-factory

ecosystem. That could materially increase both the number and longevity of MediaTek’s AI ASIC opportunities in the
longer term.

Mediatek (2454.TW)
31 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

## Document 081: 260831_daiwa_ Kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260831_daiwa_ Kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

Summary: We hosted an online investor conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 
31 August. We believe that management’s current revenue target is 15% QoQ growth in 
3Q26 (vs. market expectation of 10% QoQ). Regarding the investigation into Unimicron’s 
original place of production, the company noted that it has not seen any order shift from 
end customers. However, it believes this event will not be a negative factor for other 
substrate makers. In addition, Kinsus’ 9% share price decline today has nothing to do with 
the investigation into Unimicron. Rather, it is more likely related to its parent company’s 
plan to sell 16 million shares in Kinsus (announced last Friday). Kinsus believes this was 
driven by financial management considerations, and not by any specific intention to reduce 
its stake in Kinsus. If Kinsus’ share price continues to rise, management does not rule out 
the possibility that its parent company may trim its stake again. Overall, we believe Kinsus’ 
fundamentals remain largely in line with our expectations, with raw material prices 
continuing to rise and substrate supply tightening gradually. We maintain our Buy (1) rating 
on the stock, with a 12-month target price of TWD1,068, based on a 36x PER on the 1-
year forward EPS. Key downside risks include weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs, and AI servers.

We hosted an online conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 31 August. Overall, 
we believe the business outlook to be largely on track with our previous note (link here, 
published on 31 July). Below are our key takeaways from the conference.

Recent updates:  
• We believe the company has revised up its 3Q26 revenue growth target from 10% QoQ 
to 15% QoQ, reflecting a better-than-expected benefit from price increases. Looking 
ahead to 4Q26, management still expects growth of 10% QoQ, in line with its previous 
comments. 
• Revenue mix in 2Q26: ABF 45%, BT 38%, and contact lens 17%. Looking ahead, the 
company expects the revenue mix from ABF to gradually increase to 50–55% in 2027.

Price hike & margin expansion: 
• The company maintains its target of a 7-8% ASP increase per quarter in 2H26 to reflect 
rising costs of raw materials, including glass fibre, copper foil, drill bits, and ABF build-up 
film. 
• Although some competitors have highlighted a much larger magnitude of price hikes, 
Kinsus has not seen a similar level of increase. The company also believes the price 
hike level for ABF will be higher than that for BT in 2H26, whereas in 1H26 the price hike 
for BT was higher than that for ABF. 
• Looking into 2H26 and 2027, the company expects raw material ASPs to continue rising. 
As such, single-digit price hikes should be possible, with raw materials currently 
accounting for 30% of BT’s COGS. In addition, the driver of ASP increases is expected 
to gradually shift from raw material inflation to substrate capacity shortages.  
• In terms of gross margin expansion, Kinsus believes this will be driven mainly by an 
improved product mix and rising utilisation rates, rather than price hikes. This is similar 
to Unimicron. However, NYPCB’s gross margin expansion could be driven by internal 
price hikes within the Nanya Group.

Country

31 August 2026

MEMO 
 Key takeaways from investor conference call

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT)
Share price (31 Aug): TWD819.00 12-mth rating: Buy (1) Information Technology: Taiwan
Sheng Cheng

### Page 2

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT) 
31 August 2026 
2

Utilisation rates (UTR): 
• ABF: The company expects the UTR to increase from 85% (beginning of 2026) to 95% 
(at the end of 2026). 
• BT: The company expects the UTR to increase from 75% (beginning of 2026) to 85% (at 
the end of 2026).

Capacity expansion plan 
• Given capacity constraints, Kinsus will gradually expand its ABF capacity. It currently 
has around a 40 million ABF substrate capacity per month and plans to increase this to 
80 million by 2029. 
• According to management, the major equipment pull-in cycle will occur in 2027. After 
that, most equipment will be subsidised by end customers, and the depreciation 
expense should decline to TWD6.5-7.0bn from 2028 onward, versus TWD7.1-7.2bn in 
2026 and 2027. 
• As depreciation currently accounts for 15% of COGS, Kinsus believes it will reduce its 
selling prices slightly after clients begin subsidising the equipment, as the benefit will be 
shared between clients and Kinsus, at 7-8% each.

New growth opportunities – ABF 
• We believe Kinsus will secure a higher allocation for both CPU and GPU products from 
a global leading GPU brand in its next-generation products, given the current industry’s 
supply constraints. 
• The company also believes it will provide additional support to other GPU brands for 
their GPU products from 2027. 
• Outside of GPUs, the company sees less progress in the ASIC segment for now 
compared with GPUs. It has secured around a 10% allocation for one major ASIC 
product, but progress in other ASIC products remains relatively limited.

New growth opportunities – BT  
• The company believes pluggable optical modules and market share gains (as 
competitors gradually exit the BT market) will be key growth drivers for Kinsus. 
• According to management, the BT substrate used in optical modules will have 14 layers, 
compared with the mainstream 4-layer format today. Its existing equipment can support 
both 4-layer and 14-layer BT substrates. However, the company may need to add some 
routing capacity to support optical module demand.

## Document 085: 260901_gs_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/260901_gs_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-01
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

leaving headroom unused, and no target or ceiling has been set for Nvidia’s eventual
holding.
Partnership expanded across AI infrastructure, local AI computing and n
automotive: Alongside the pricing, Nvidia and MediaTek announced a deepening of
their longstanding collaboration spanning three areas, including 1) AI
infrastructure: MediaTek will adopt Nvidia’s NVLink Fusion platform to enable
customers to develop custom AI infrastructure designed to integrate with Nvidia

rack-scale systems and AI factories. 2) Local AI computing: the companies will
continue to collaborate on multiple generations of RTX Spark™ and DGX Spark™ PC
chips integrating Nvidia GPUs with MediaTek SoCs. 3) Automotive: MediaTek and
Nvidia will continue developing platforms for AI-powered, software-defined vehicles
in the era of physical AI.
Key highlights from the joint interview
Following the announcement, the CEOs of Nvidia and MediaTek were interviewed jointly

by Bloomberg TV. Below we summarize key highlights from the interview.
Nvidia characterized the partnership with MediaTek as a multi-generation and n
long-term collaboration. Nvidia commented that the two companies are working
together across multiple generations of edge AI and PC SoCs, while the expanded
partnership will also cover data-center XPUs, networking and robotic systems.
Nvidia added that it is a decade-long roadmap.
The expanded collaboration will allow MediaTek’s XPUs to connect directly into n

Nvidia’s broader AI factory architecture. Nvidia noted that it will connect its
NVLink Fusion scale-up system, Spectrum-X switches and broader networking
infrastructure to MediaTek’s XPUs.  According to Nvidia, this would allow MediaTek
XPUs to be integrated into data centers already running Nvidia infrastructure, while
MediaTek XPU project wins could also create opportunities for Nvidia networking
products.
MediaTek expects the partnership to support growth in its AI ASIC business, n

although the new revenue contribution will take time. When asked whether the
collaboration could accelerate MediaTek’s AI chip business, MediaTek said that it
expects this to happen, but noted that it would take some time as the company is
only beginning to integrate Nvidia’s latest NVLink version into the relevant chassis.
MediaTek highlighted faster time-to-market for customers as a key benefit.
Both companies indicated that customer engagement around NVLink Fusion has n

already started. Nvidia said the collaboration was in large part driven by customer
requests and that there are already customers committed to NVLink Fusion.
Nvidia positioned NVLink Fusion as a way to accommodate custom XPUs within n
its existing infrastructure rather than as a replacement for its broader platform.
Nvidia noted that XPUs are already present in the market and said it is opening its
platform to allow customers’ specialized XPUs to connect more easily to Nvidia

infrastructure. Nvidia added that it expects to continue participating through
networking, switching and CPUs, even in AI factories where some racks incorporate
XPUs.
1 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

Nvidia indicated that the partnership enables MediaTek to offer a more n
complete end-to-end XPU solution. Nvidia noted that an XPU provider would
otherwise need to address multiple system-level components, including NVLink
switching, NICs, Ethernet switching and scale-up/scale-out networking. Through the
partnership, these components can be provided through Nvidia’s existing
infrastructure.
MediaTek plans to use the bond proceeds across supply-chain and technology n

investments. MediaTek said supply-chain investment will be one use of proceeds
and emphasized that the company’s supply chain is global. The company also plans
to invest further in design capabilities, ASIC chip capabilities, IP and interconnects,
and advanced packaging, with the proceeds supporting the expanded Nvidia
collaboration.
The collaboration also extends to Nvidia’s custom HBM architecture. Nvidia n
indicated that its next-generation custom HBM will use an Nvidia-designed base

layer that has been extended to NVLink Fusion partners, including MediaTek and
Amazon. Nvidia noted that the NVHBM and XPU die will be integrated into a CoWoS
package, allowing partners to leverage Nvidia’s installed base and supply-chain
scale.
MediaTek identified the supply chain as its primary bottleneck. MediaTek said n
supply-chain challenges extend beyond HBM to substrates and other components,
while noting that its location in Taiwan and close collaboration with Nvidia are

helpful. Nvidia added that its networking ecosystem is becoming part of MediaTek’s
supply chain, while MediaTek’s XPU is becoming part of Nvidia’s supply chain.
Nvidia sees a solid standalone business case in its MediaTek investment. In n
response to questions on financing, Nvidia noted that MediaTek operates its own
independent business and is already a highly profitable and successful company.
Nvidia indicated the investment is grounded in the companies’ deep, multi-year

technology partnership, with the current roadmap extending over a decade, and is
intended to demonstrate Nvidia’s confidence in and commitment to the
collaboration. Nvidia also expects the investment to generate attractive returns.
Our thoughts - Strong ecosystem endorsement, broader system-level
opportunity and potential new TAM into custom HBM base die
Strong endorsement from two leading AI ecosystem partners
We believe the most immediate takeaway is the strong endorsement from both Nvidia

and Alphabet. Nvidia subscribed to US$3.5bn of MediaTek’s US$3.9bn CB issuance,
while Alphabet and global institutional investors participated in the remaining
US$400mn. In our view, Nvidia’s capital commitment provides strong validation of
MediaTek’s silicon, connectivity and advanced packaging capabilities. Alphabet’s
participation also reinforces MediaTek’s relevance across multiple major AI ecosystems.
Funding should strengthen supply-chain security and support continued technology

investment. According to the company, one of the original purposes of the financing
was to secure capacity and supply for constrained components such as HBM.
Although certain customers currently procure HBM and other components directly, we
1 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

believe MediaTek could assume greater supply-chain responsibility under future
engagement models, including entering into capacity or supply agreements on behalf of
customer projects.
The company also indicated that it plans to invest further in its technology and design
capabilities, including ASIC design, IP , interconnects and advanced packaging, etc. We
believe the additional funding should provide MediaTek with greater flexibility to
secure the resources required for increasingly complex AI related programs.

The partnership broadens MediaTek’s role from custom AI silicon toward system-level
enablement
Based on the interview and the company announcement, we believe MediaTek is seeking
to extend its role beyond designing and delivering an individual AI ASIC toward helping
customers address a broader portion of the system surrounding the XPU. This could
include connectivity, memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and
integration with rack-scale AI infrastructure.

However, we do not believe this means MediaTek intends to build or sell complete
AI racks. Rather, MediaTek can help customers integrate their custom XPUs into
Nvidia’s pre-validated chassis, networking and rack-scale architectures, allowing
customers to deploy and scale their AI infrastructure more efficiently.
NVLink Fusion is intended to provide customers with a faster path from custom XPU to
AI factory. Customers can focus their resources on the differentiated compute portion of

the accelerator while relying on Nvidia and MediaTek for surrounding technologies such
as NVLink connectivity, high-speed SerDes, memory architecture, packaging,
manufacturing and system qualification.
Instead of independently engineering and qualifying every component surrounding the
custom XPU, customers can use NVIDIA ’s prebuilt, prequalified and system-prevalidated
platform as a starting point. We believe this should reduce integration complexity and

risk, shorten time to market and make it easier for customers to deploy custom XPUs
within existing Nvidia-based data centers and scale them into rack-level AI factories.
Incremental revenue will likely take time, but the partnership expands MediaTek’s
longer-term opportunity
MediaTek and Nvidia did not provide a timeline for the first NVLink Fusion-related
customer, product or revenue contribution. The company indicated that it is only
beginning to integrate the latest version of NVLink, after which customers would still

need to complete their own product development, qualification and deployment
processes. We therefore see limited immediate earnings implications and believe the
incremental opportunity is more likely to emerge over the medium to long term.
Nevertheless, the partnership broadens MediaTek’s addressable opportunity from
designing an individual AI ASIC toward enabling a production-ready system. We
believe this could improve customer win rates and increase MediaTek’s potential content

and value capture per project through additional contributions from connectivity,
memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and system integration.
1 September 2026   4
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 5

The relationship extends across the full edge-to-cloud AI computing stack 
Beyond AI infrastructure, the partnership covers multiple generations of DGX Spark and 
RTX Spark chips for local AI computing, as well as automotive platforms. We therefore 
view the announcement as a broader, multi-generation technology alignment spanning 
cloud AI factories, enterprise and local AI computing, PCs and physical AI, rather than a 
single-product collaboration. 
Management noted that the Nvidia partnership should not restrict MediaTek’s existing 
custom ASIC engagements. We see NVLink Fusion as an additional architecture option 
rather than an exclusive platform. Customers will be able to decide whether to adopt the 
Nvidia ecosystem depending on their infrastructure and workload requirements, while 
MediaTek can continue supporting customers using other architectures. 
In our view, Alphabet’s participation in the CB issuance should allay any concerns 
some investors may have had that the closer Nvidia relationship could affect 
MediaTek’s existing hyperscaler engagements. Instead, we believe MediaTek’s 
positioning is to remain architecture-flexible while providing customers with a broader 
range of design and system-integration options. 
NVHBM collaboration extends MediaTek’s participation into the memory and 
packaging architecture 
We note that Nvidia’s next-generation NVHBM will use a custom HBM base layer 
designed by Nvidia, which has also been extended to NVLink Fusion partners including 
MediaTek and Amazon. Nvidia noted that NVHBM will be integrated with the XPU die in 
a CoWoS package, allowing partners to leverage NVIDIA ’s broader HBM and packaging 
supply chain. While the NVHBM base layer itself is NVIDIA-designed, we believe 
MediaTek’s participation could deepen its involvement in memory architecture, 
interconnect and advanced packaging integration for XPU programs. Separately, 
MediaTek has highlighted custom HBM and memory-interface capabilities as part 
of its broader XPU offering, which we believe could provide incremental content 
opportunities for customer-specific designs outside Nvidia’s standardized NVHBM 
implementation. 
Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - MediaTek 
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application 
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to 
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor, 
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive 
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to 
achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by 
43%/59% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1) 
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G 
flagship SoCs), 2) strong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in the 
automotive/computing sectors.

## Document 088: 260902_daiwa_NYPCB

Source PDF: all report pdf/260902_daiwa_NYPCB.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We hosted an online conference call for NYPCB on 2 
September, after trading hours. Overall, we believe the price hike trend is 
largely on track with our estimates despite a short hiccup in 4Q26E (for 
more details, please refer to our earlier note, Escalating price hike in 
substrates). Looking ahead, we believe the trend towards enlarging body 
sizes and multiple core layers in ABF substrates and the supply-demand 
imbalance in BT substrates will be key growth drivers for NYPCB.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. We are comfortable with our 
revenue growth estimate of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26E, given ongoing price 
hikes and a rising utilisation rate at Kunshan (likely to reach 100% in 
4Q26E). We believe the yield rate at Kunshan still has room to improve, 
which could negatively impact the gross margin (GM) performance in 
3Q26E. That said, compared with July, we expect the GM to improve 6-7pp 
MoM in August given the rising yield rate, and remain confident of a c.30% 
GM in 3Q26E. We expect NYPCB to achieve higher price hikes than other 
substrate vendors, as it does not sign long-term agreements with end 
customers. However, NYPCB has stated that it currently has no intention to 
hike BT substrate prices further from 4Q26, as raw material price hikes 
have eased recently; as such, we have accounted for it in our model. 
Lastly, NYPCB mentioned that it has received new AI HDI orders from a 
US-based ODM, which will result in its PCB segment reaching c.30% QoQ 
revenue growth and operating profit from 4Q26E.

Long-term investment thesis. We believe a meaningful spec upgrade is 
also likely to occur in ABF substrates, as core layer counts will need to 
increase their rigidity to handle the increasing body size of substrates. This 
trend would digest more ABF capacity and relevant raw materials (eg, T-
glass) than just unit growth from 2028E. Also, we believe this would make 
the current upcycle last longer than previous ones. As for BT substrates, 
we believe ASP growth will accelerate again as two key players are 
gradually reducing their BT capacities. Thus, we maintain our BT ASP 
assumption for 2027E despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E.

What we recommend: While we lower the 2026E EPS by 4% on a 
reduced BT price hike in 4Q26E, we keep the 2027-28E EPS largely 
unchanged. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12-month TP to 
TWD2,460 (from TWD2,444), based on an unchanged target PER of 49x 
applied to the 1-year forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-41% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on ABF/BT ASP hikes.

2 September 2026

Company profile 
Nan Ya PCB originated as one of the business divisions of Nan Ya Plastics Corporation before 
transforming into a subsidiary in 1997, specialising in the manufacturing of printed circuit boards (PCBs) 
and integrated circuit (IC) substrates. The company’s primary end applications include networking 
(c.50%), PCs (c.20%), consumer electronics (c.10%), automotive (c.10%) and other sectors. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Ya PCB's board of directors (BoD) is responsible for overseeing the company's operations
and ensuring adherence to corporate governance principles. The board comprises individuals
with diverse expertise, contributing to effective decision-making and strategic planning. The
company emphasizes the importance of corporate governance as a set of processes, customs,
policies, laws, and institutions affecting the way the corporation is directed, administered, or
controlled. Principal stakeholders include stockholders, management, and the board of directors,
among others.
Capital management 2
Nan Ya PCB maintains a prudent approach to capital management, ensuring financial stability
and the availability of resources necessary for operational and strategic initiatives. The
company's financial statements reflect a solid capital structure, supporting its growth and
sustainability objectives.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to strict policies regarding related party transactions to maintain
transparency and fairness. Such transactions are conducted under terms comparable to those
with independent third parties and are disclosed in financial reports to ensure compliance with
regulatory standards and corporate governance best practices. For example, the company's
consolidated financial statements provide details on transactions with related parties, ensuring
transparency and accountability.
S Supply chain
management 1
Nan Ya PCB is committed to sustainable supply chain management, emphasizing environmental
protection, procurement policies, and social responsibility. The company has established a
comprehensive procurement policy that integrates sustainability considerations, ensuring that
suppliers adhere to ethical standards and environmental regulations. This commitment is part of
the company's broader sustainable responsibility policy, which includes areas such as corporate
governance, environmental protection, and social welfare.
E Data security 1
While specific details on Nan Ya PCB's data security measures are not extensively disclosed in
the available sources, the company recognizes the importance of information security in its
operations. As part of its corporate governance framework, Nan Ya PCB is likely to implement
standard data protection practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance
with relevant regulations. Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official
communications or contact the company directly for detailed information on data security
policies.

### Page 1

M
Update
Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Credo's
FY1Q27 Results
We see positive implications for Bizlink from Credo's comments
on expanding AEC   and emerging ALC opportunities.
FY1Q27 (ended July 2026) results:
• Credo (CRDO.US, not covered) reported revenue of US$479mn (+10% QoQ/
+115% YoY), roughly in line with the consensus of US$474mn and guidance
of US$465-475mn.
• Non-GAAP GM was 68% (-0.3ppt QoQ) in line with consensus at 68% and

guidance at 67-69%.
• Non-GAAP NP was US$236mn (+4% QoQ/+140% YoY), roughly in line with
consensus at US$232mn.
• Four customers each contributed more than 10% of Credo's revenue in
FY1Q27, i.e., 33%, 28%, 13% and 10%, respectively.
FY2Q27 and FY27 outlook:
•  FY2Q27 revenue is guided to be US$525-535mn (+11% QoQ/+98% YoY at the
midpoint, largely in line with consensus of US$526mn), non-GAAP GM of 67-
69% (vs. consensus at 67.5%) and opex of US$100-105mn.

• For FY27, Credo now expects total revenue to grow >85% YoY (vs.
consensus at ~86% YoY), with an  inflection in 2H mainly from optical
revenue (>US$600mn for FY27 from Zero-Flap Optics, silicon photonics PICs
and optical DSPs, with each to contribute >US$100mn).
• Credo expects its FY27 GM to stay flat YoY, while operating expenses might
increase by ~55% YoY.
• 1.6T AEC might start to emerge from 2HFY27.
• ALCs and OmniConnect gearboxes remain on track for initial revenue from

FY28, management believes both are multi-billion dollar opportunities for
Credo.
• It also sees increasing customer activity on NPO for scale up network, with
confirmed PIC design wins for ramp in FY28.
Bizlink Read-across:
• We see positive implications from Credo's comments on the AEC's spec
upgrade and its penetration into more hyperscalers and neoclouds, as well as
the reiterated timing of ALC ramp in FY28.
• We stay constructive on Bizlink for its multiple drivers in the AI deployment

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Bizlink (3665.TW)
Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We think our target P/E multiple is justified by an
earnings CAGR of 46% seen over 2025-28e, which implies a PEG of around 0.7. This is in line
with the average of 0.7 in 2019-24, when the stock was trading at an average 13x P/E for a 19%
earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Faster and wider AEC adoption
n Data center and semi equipment capex increases that drive stronger HPC and semi-cap

business
n Stronger recovery for automotive business
Risks to Downside
n Lower-than-expected AEC adoption
n Intensifying competition
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower-than-expected growth in the semi capital equipment market
n Continued weakness in the auto industry

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

台新新光金 (2887.TT)

季獲利走勢與年成長率

研究員/陳晏平 TINA.YP.CHEN@uni-psg.com

1H26 稅後淨利為 438 億元，成長 328%YOY，EPS 為 1.69 元。獲利
成長主要因:(1)台新銀核心收益皆逾兩成的成長 ， 該子公司獲利成
長 26%YOY 至 124 億元；(2)2025/7/24 完成合併新光金，新光銀、
元富證 1H26 合計額外貢獻 67 億元獲利，加上 2026 年接軌 IFRS 
17，壽險開始有穩定利差益，1H26 合併後壽險子公司的獲利為 225
億元。展望 2026~2027 年，由於:(1) 台新銀維持放款將高個位數
成長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修 2026 年淨利息收益至成長
19%YOY，並預期 2027 年將成長 5%YOY；(2) 台新銀財管商品銷售
動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期 2026 年整體淨手續費收益
將雙位數成長 ，2027 年個位數增加 ；(3) 台新銀資產品質穩定 ， 但
為新增放款 1%/1.5%法定提存，預期2026~2027 年淨提存將增加六
成至 30 億元 ；(4) 新壽接軌 IFRS 17 後擁穩定利差益 ， 另有80~100
億元的收購價格分攤數額外挹注，推估 2026 年將貢獻獲利逾 400
億元 ，2027 年亦將持續穩增；(5) 台股價量齊揚 ， 帶動證券子公司
獲利明顯增長，但同時也墊高基期。因此，在全年的合併效果下，
預估台新新光金 2026~2027 年稅後淨利為 805 億元、798 億元，成
長 116%、 衰退1%YOY， 扣除特別股股利25 億元後 ，EPS 為 3.11 元、
3.08 元 。 投資建議方面 ， 雖2026 年獲利明顯跳增 ， 且新壽1H26 已
累積 512 億元 OCI 資本利得亦可為配息來源，預期 2027 年現金股
利將優於 2026 年的 1 元水準 ， 不過2026 年獲利提升有部份是來自
無實際現金流入的 PPA，加上人壽能否上繳股利至金控尚不明確，
因此，在銀行、證券子公司較能上繳股利情況下，現金股利提升幅
度恐將受限。另以2027 年每股淨值 30 元、ROE 10~11%來看，目前
股價已逾 1.3xPBR，評價相對合理，故對台新新光金持「中立」投
資建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 台新銀放款將高個位數成 長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修
2026年淨利息收益至成長19%YOY， 並預期2027年將成長5%YOY 
1H26底台新銀放款餘額為2.027兆元，成長11%YTD、12%YOY，
主要成長動能來自法人金融 、 加值型房屋貸款 、 個人無擔信用
貸款，分別增加15.5%、10%、15%YOY，一般房屋貸款則 小增
2%YOY。利差方面，雖高利差外幣放款成長動能強勁，1H26外
幣放款成長33%YOY，有提高資金運用效率 ，但受到台灣市場資
金緊俏影響，導致資金成本上揚，因此，2Q26存放利差為

1.53%，縮小6bpsQOQ、擴大14bpsYOY，淨利差亦同步 縮小
6bpsQOQ、擴大13bpsYOY至1.42%。不過因放款YOY持續成長、
淨利差YOY亦呈現擴大趨勢，1H26台新銀淨利息收益為200億
元，成長27%YOY。

圖一、台新銀利差趨勢

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，由於出口動能維持強勁，景氣持續成長，
台新銀維持原預期—法金放款雙位數增長 、個金放款穩健高
個位數成長 ， 預期台新銀2026年整體放款將高個位數成長 。但
因2027年即將與新光銀合併 ， 預期將有部分法金客戶重疊 ， 須
重新評估放款額度 ， 預期2027年放款成長性將會不如2026年。
利差方面 ， 雖近期台灣資金緊俏影響資金成本 ， 但因先前美國
降息刺激高利差外幣放款需求，在 1H26存放款結構調整優於
預期下，公司上修2026年淨利差擴大幅度至5~8bpsYOY(原預
期將擴大4~5bpsYOY)。 因此 ， 研究部上修台新銀2026年淨利息
收益2%至404億元，成長19%YOY，並預期2027年將續增5%YOY。

2. 台新銀財管商品銷售動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期
2026年整體淨手續費收益將雙位數成長，2027年個位數增加 
1H26台新銀整體淨手續費收益為110億元，成長32%YOY，主要
成長動能來自:(1)投資市場火熱刺激財富管理商品熱銷，整
體財富管理業務手續費收益成長32%YOY；(2)透過簽帳金額結
構改變(減少低/零手收)、控制權益支出，信用卡業務手續費
收入增加28%YOY；(3)隨企金放款增長，企金相關手續費增加
56%YOY。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

展望2026~2027年，因投資市場持續位在高檔，仍有利財富管
理商品銷售 ， 因此 ， 公司維持2026年台新銀整體淨手續費收益
將持續雙位數成長的預期，研究部推估將落在210~215億元水
準 ， 成長逾兩成 。 而在基期墊高下 ， 研究部預期2027年整體淨
手續費收益將個位數穩增。

3. 台新銀資產品質穩定，但為新增放款1%/1.5%法定提存，預期
2026~2027年淨提存將增加六成至30億元 
2026年以來無大額不良壞帳產生 ， 台新銀1H26底逾放比 、 呆帳
覆蓋率分別為0.13%、923%，資產品質不錯，些微優於產業平
均逾放比0.14%、呆帳覆蓋率940%。提存方面，雖針對新增逾
放的額外提存不多 ， 但因放款成長動能不弱 ， 而有新增放款的
1%或1.5%正常提存需求 ， 台新銀1H26淨呆帳提存為25億元 ，增
加112%YOY。

圖二、台新銀資產品質趨勢與同業平均水準比較

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，目前資產品質穩定，暫無潛在大型不良放
款產生 ， 但因仍持續有新增放款的1%或1.5%正常提存， 公司預
期台新銀2026年信用成本將在10~15bps區間(維持不變)，較
2025年的10bps略增。研究部按公司預期的區間中上緣進行推
估，估計台新銀2026~2027年淨呆帳提存為30億元，較2025年
增加近六成。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

4. 新壽接軌IFRS 17後擁穩定利差益，另有80~100億元的收購價
格分攤數額外挹注，推估2026年將貢獻獲利逾400億元，2027
年持續穩增 
台新金與新光金合併案已在2025/7/24完成，台新金為存續公
司 ， 並更名為台新新光金 。 而旗下兩家投信子公司 、 兩家壽險
子公司、兩家證券子公司已分別在 2025/10/24、2026/1/1、
2026/4/6完成整併，並計畫2027/1/1完成兩家銀行子公司間
的整併。

其中 ， 兩家壽險子公司合併後 ， 台新人壽為存續公司 ， 但更名
為新光人壽。因2026年接軌IFRS 17，現階段壽險公司主要獲
利來源包括：保險服務結果、財務結果、其他營業結果。在保
險服務結果方面，1H26共釋出95億元CSM，加計RA釋出、實際
理賠及費用與預期的差異 ， 保險服務結果共140.5億元 。 此外 ，
美債殖利率仍位在高檔，新錢投資報酬率不差，且在
2025/7/24合併當下有針對投資資產進行重新評價，投資資產
規模因而明顯下降， 1H26避險前經常性收益率為 4.19%，較
2025年提升85bpsYOY。(若以同樣投資資產規模基礎，還原後
為3.43%，仍為提高趨勢) 另，受惠避險工具費用減降，避險
成本下滑至1.22%， 以及投資市場上漲 ， 多少帶動TPL投資部位
評價利益、處分利益增加，1H26避險後投資報酬率為3.73%。
(若加計OCI資本利得則為5.85%)負債成本則因接軌IFRS 17，
保單利息成本如預期降至2.3%，換算1H26利差益為1.43%，財
務結果貢獻179億元。再扣除其他營業結果、稅負等，1H26新
光人壽獲利為225億元。

展望2026~2027年，由於1H26新增351億元CSM(2026年目標為
700億元)，1H26底帳上CSM餘額仍有2936億元，若以1H26攤銷
率推估 ， 預期2026年將可釋出約200億元的CSM至損益表上。加
上公司預期經常性收益率可提升至 3.5%以上、保單成本亦有
望明顯降低 ， 往後可享有較穩定的利差益 ， 且持續有收購價格
分攤數(PPA)額外挹注(原先新壽AC項下的債券部位具有較大
額未實現損失 ， 在合併當下是採公允價值評價 ， 隨此些債券陸
續到期 ， 原本帳上未實現損失可望逐步攤銷出來 ， 並挹注至損
益表上，亦即為收購價格分攤數)，此次法說會公司揭露2026
年PPA將達80~100億元 ， 推估新壽2026年獲利將可逾400億元 。
而因新壽TPL部位佔比不高，且隨CSM穩定釋出，並持續有PPA
額外貢獻，預期2027年獲利將穩定成長。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

至於新光銀方面，1H26在淨利息收益穩增3%YOY、淨手續費收
益大增25%YOY、合併相關費用明顯減少、淨提存略有增加下，
獲利顯著大增168%YOY至48億元 。展望2026~2027年 ， 公司預期
2026年放款將個位數成長 、 淨利差維持穩定 ， 信用成本亦維持
原先預期的15~18bps，但淨手續費收益將雙位數成長(原預期
為中個位數增長)，加上營業費用正常化，因此，研究部上修
新光銀2026年獲利貢獻達96億元水準。

5. 台新新光金2027年現金股利將有望優於2026年，但人壽能否
上繳獲利至金控仍未可知，現金股利提升幅度恐不如預期 
資本適足率方面 ，旗下的台新銀、 新光銀1H26底整體資本適足
率、第一類資本適足率各為 13.7%、12.0%，以及14.24%、
12.41%，資本充足無虞。而合併後的新光人壽在 2026年接軌
TIS 時，已向主管機關申請在 125% ，而 1H26 底 約 莫 落 在
125~130%區間水準，在法定下限—100%之上。

圖三、台新金、台新銀資本適足率情形

*金控整體資本適足率僅揭露至1Q26底 
資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

股利政策方面 ，過去為保留資本因應業務成長 ，原台新金現金
股利配發率普遍落在35~50%區間水準 ， 另搭配些許股票股利 ，
整體股利配發率約莫在65~90%區間水準。但 因台新金為合併
新光金，台新新光金發行高達118.9億股的普通股來因應此次
合併案，使得台新新光金普通股股本較原先大幅膨脹九成 ， 因
此，為控制股本 、 提升股利競爭力 ，公司傾向往後股利政策將
以現金股利為主 ， 並會隨著獲利慢慢增加 ， 不會波動過大。雖：
(1)2026年在接軌IFRS 17後，新壽獲利已相對穩定；(2)前述
的收購價格分攤數(PPA)雖無實際現金流入，但依然可進行分
配；(3)2026年以來股市顯著上漲，1H26新壽已累積512億元

OCI股票資本利得，亦可成為分配來源，預期2027年現金股利
將較2026年的1元提升。但人壽是否能上繳股利至金控尚不明
確， 目前主管機關僅提出概略性方向 ， 實施細則還未出爐 ， 因
此 ， 在銀行 、 證券子公司較能上繳股利情況下 ， 現金股利提升
幅度恐怕有限。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或

聯亞 公布 InP 基 板 長 約 ， 並 加 碼 投 資 MOCVD 設 備 加 大 擴 產 力 道：  
聯亞 公 布 與 美 、 日 InP 基 板 大 廠 簽 訂 四 年 供 應 長 約 ， 看 好 AI 驅 動 高 速 光 通
訊 需 求，並 再 次 投 資 9.9 億元 買 入 德 國 大 廠 MOCVD 設備 加 大 擴 產 力 道，顯
示 看 好 未 來 AI 基 建 成 長 力 道 ，持 續 擴 產 CW Laser 的 決 心 。

Sipho -Based 光 收 發 模 組 相 對 EML 光 收 發 模 組 具 有 成 本 優 勢 ， 進 一 步 推 升
CW 雷 射 的 需 求；由 於 新 竹 的 CW 雷 射 後 段 合 作 夥 伴 經 歷 多 次 嘗 試 後 終 於 順
利 擴 產 ， 推 動 CW Chip 出 貨 量 大 幅 增 長 ， 持 續 推 升 營 收 成 長 ， 預 估 3Q26
營收 15.36 億 元，QoQ+25.81% ，稅 後 淨 利 5.55 億 元，QoQ+18.52% ，EPS5.45
元 。  
展望 2026 年， AI 需 求 帶 動 CW Laser 需 求 持 續 增 加 ， 受 惠 於 新 增 北 美 CSP
客戶以及 CW Epi 客 戶 需 求 大 幅 增 加 ， 預 期2026 年 矽 光 相 關 營 收 可 望 為 2025
年的 3 倍 ， 隨 著 公 司 持 續 擴 產 ，2027 年 底 產 能 將 較 2026 年 底 成 長 100% 以
上 ， 且 進 一 步 帶 動 2028 年 產 能 擴 張 ； 藉 著 與 住 友 電 工 簽 訂 5 年 長 期 磷 化 銦
基板合約，將可望降低中國管制稀土帶來的不確定性，預估 2026 年營收
53.19 億 元，YoY+141.48% ，稅 後 淨 利 19.53 億 元，YoY+355.82% ，EPS19.18
元 。

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 8 月 27 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投資評等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為三個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」 及 「 買 進 」 均 有 upside 目 標 價 。 兩 者 差 別 僅 在 於 ， 首 次 評 等
時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「買進」及「強力買進」之投資評等，就是責任的
開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ，
直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

 投資評價與建議

獻，挹 注未來成長動 能， 研究部看好公司未來 產品 需求將穩定成長，預估
2026/2027 EPS 6.15/8.13 元 ， 給予歷史本益比區間中值40X，依台特化 2027 EPS

### Page 2

2

台特化為半導體特殊氣體及半導體化學材料專業開發製造商：台特化成立於 2013
年，中美晶於2018 年入股，持股30.93%。公司總部及生產工廠位於彰濱工業區，
產品自製比例高，自製產品包含矽乙烷（Disilane）、矽丙烷（Trisilane） 、無水氟
化氫（AHF） ，代理產品則包含矽甲烷（Silane） 、其餘海外代理產品。公司 2Q26
特氣營收比重約 36%，清潔營收比重約 42%，表面精密鍍膜（Coating）及新品
營收比重約 19%。 
2Q26 獲利低於預期：台特化 2Q26 營收 8.9 億元， （+4.3％ QoQ，+240.0％ 
YoY） ，低於預期，主因特殊氣體營收成長幅度未如預期，毛利率51.91%，（+0.95 
pts. QoQ，-6.41 pts. YoY） ，低於預期，主因特殊氣體營收比例較預期低，產品組
合較預期差，且因弘潔擴廠致使前期人力成本增加，影響毛利率表現所致，稅後淨
利 2.2 億元（+12.8% QoQ，+89.7％ YoY） ，稅後EPS 1.5 元，低於預期。 
台特化計畫擴產，推升未來營運動能：台特化目前特氣稼動率已達 9 成，公司啟動
擴產計畫， 矽乙烷年產能將從原先30 噸提升至 40 噸 ， 預計將於4Q26 開始動工，
預期 2Q27 完工，並於 3Q27 投產貢獻，因屬於原有產線的擴充，將不須再進行
客戶認證；AHF 增設一條產線，年產能從 10 噸提升至 20 噸，預計 1Q27 開始動
工，預期2Q27-3Q27 完工，並於4Q27 投產貢獻。因應特氣需求成長，公司亦啟
動鋼瓶容器擴增採購計畫，預期將於 2Q27 開始進行首批交付，並於 4Q27 全數
交付完畢。 
弘潔各廠區別營收佔比為中國 30%、新竹廠 50%、台南廠 20%。因客戶需求強
勁，弘潔產能已達滿載，啟動新竹廠的新廠建置及台南廠產線擴產計畫。新竹廠新
廠預計將於 1Q27 進行產線測試，預期 2Q27 正式量產營運，預計新竹廠超高潔
淨清潔產能將翻倍；台南廠超高潔淨清潔產能擴充計畫則目前正在進行客戶驗證
中，產能提升約 33%，預期將於 4Q26 驗證通過並導入量產貢獻營收。而公司表
面精密鍍膜需求同樣強勁，公司於新竹廠新廠、台南廠新產線皆有擴充計畫，預期
皆將於 2Q27 完工投產，屆時表面精密鍍膜產能將提升至目前產能的250%。隨著
台特化、弘潔產能持續擴充，將持續挹注台特化未來營運成長動能。 
產品需求穩定成長，維持買進投資建議：展望後市，隨著半導體製程演進及產能擴
充，市場規模擴大，對於特殊氣體、清洗、Coating 需求量預期將持續提升，AHF
預期也將持續放量貢獻營收，帶動獲利表現成長。短期因受限於弘潔產能已滿載，
預期弘潔營收成長幅度將趨緩 ， 抑制短期營收增幅， 然隨著公司各項擴建案陸續完
工投產，將有利提升營收及獲利表現。綜合以上，研究部看好台特化未來產品需求

營運現況與分析

個股報告 
宜鼎(5289) 
持續創造價值 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $2485 
前次投資建議：2026.04.17： 
「買進」 ，目標價: $1400

報告日期：2026.08.28 研 究 員：王穎

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 需求強勁帶動記憶體報價持續供不應求有利記憶體報價持續上漲，且邊緣 AI 需求持續成
長，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價 2485 元(5x 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 66.8%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/27 收盤價： 1,490.00 ➢ 26Q2 營收 224.4 億元，QoQ+70.3%、YoY+641.1%，毛
利率 65.0%，QoQ+5.9ppts，營業費用 14.4 億元，營業
利益 131.5 億，業外收入 6,900 萬元，稅後淨利 103.68
億元，YoY+5493.8%，EPS 108.93 元。營收與毛利皆成
長主要受惠記憶體漲價及出貨量雙雙成長，創下歷史單季
新高。 
➢ 展望 26Q3 受惠記憶體緊缺報價持續上漲，且宜鼎銷售主
要為 AI server、工控、AI solutions 等高附加價值相關產
品，因此記憶體大幅度漲價仍可將成本轉嫁於客戶，營收
規模將持續創高 。因此兆豐預估營收 295.4 億元，
QoQ+31.6%，YoY+675.7%，毛利率 59.6%，稅後淨利
122.96 億元，YoY+1812.1%，EPS 為 128.08 元。 
➢ 展望 2026~2027 年，隨AI 需求爆炸性成長，記憶體呈現
供不應求，將推升記憶體價格持續走高，同時 AI 快速發
展下，Edge AI 需求將快速成長，預期 AI 相關產品占比
將持續提高，有助於宜鼎業績將持續成長，續創高峰。預
估 2026~2027 年 營 收 974.7/1426.2 億元，
YoY+583.5/46.3%，毛利率 60.8/53.4%，稅後淨利
409.7/477.2 億 元 ，YoY+1912.4/16.5% ， EPS 為
426.7/497.1 元。 
➢ 宜鼎目前位於 3 X 27(F)PER)，低於過往 5~22X PER，
維持 「買進 」投資評等， 上調 目標價 2485 元 (5x 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 66.8%。

營 運 分 析

宜鼎為全球著名的記憶體廠商 
宜鼎國際股份有限公司成立於 2005 年 3 月，是一家以服務為導向的工業用
儲存裝置供應商，專注於企業級、工業、航太、醫療與國防等應用市場，提
供工業用嵌入式儲存裝置與工業用動態隨機記憶體模組與相關技術服務，並
以自有品牌 「Innodisk」 行銷全球。26Q2 產品組合：DRAM 61%、Flash 34%、
AI solution 4%、Others 1%。

26Q2 獲利創高受惠記憶體報價持續成長 
26Q2 營收 224.4 億元， QoQ+70.3%、YoY+641.1%，毛利率 65.0%，
QoQ+5.9ppts，營業費用 14.4 億元，營業利益131.5 億，業外收入6,900 萬
元，稅後淨利 103.68 億元，YoY+5493.8%，EPS 108.93 元。營收與毛利皆
成長主要受惠記憶體漲價及出貨量雙雙成長，創下歷史單季新高。

26Q3 DRAM、NAND報價持續上漲，具備轉嫁能力獲利將創高 
展望 26Q3 受惠記憶體緊缺報價持續上漲，且宜鼎銷售主要為 AI server、工
控、AI solutions 等高附加價值相關產品，因此記憶體大幅度漲價仍可將成本
轉嫁於客戶，營收規模將持續創高。因此兆豐預估營收 295.4 億元，
QoQ+31.6%，YoY+675.7%，毛利率  59.6%，稅後淨利 122.96 億元，
YoY+1812.1%，EPS為 128.08 元。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
圖三、2027營收持續創高  
宜鼎各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：宜鼎；兆豐投顧預估

記憶體持續供不應求，報價漲價不止 
展望 2026~2027 年，隨 AI 需求爆炸性成長，記憶體呈現供不應求，將推升
記憶體價格持續走高，同時 AI 快速發展下，Edge AI需求將快速成長，預期
AI 相關產品占比將持續提高，有助於宜鼎業績將持續成長，續創高峰。預估
2026~2027 年營收 974.7/1426.2 億元， YoY+583.5/46.3%，毛利率
60.8/53.4%，稅後淨利 409.7/477.2 億元， YoY+1912.4/16.5%，EPS 為
426.7/497.1 元。

邊緣 AI帶動記憶體產業回溫，維持「買進」投資評等 
宜鼎目前位於 3 X 27(F)PER) 低於過往 5~22X PER，考量記憶體受到 AI 需
求爆炸性成長，預期將供不應求延續至 2027 年，將推升記憶體價格持續上
漲，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價 2485 元(7x 26(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間
66.8%。

### Page 1

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/09/01 投資速報

電子–ABF 設備 
長廣 (7795.TT) 
訂單成長近 6 倍，公司積極提升擴產速度與幅度

買進(維持評等) 
出刊緣由：更新近況

個股與大盤走勢

加權指數報酬率 5.4 3.9 29.8 87.5 
個股股價報酬率 35.4 33.9 32.8

營收走勢圖

研究員/張書明 brady.chang@uni-psg.com

研究部維持長廣買進投資建議，係因： （1）推估在手訂單 45 億元
（+580%YoY） ，已達 25 年營收的 2.5 倍，能見度延續至 2030 年；
（2）2027 年產能成長 +63%，2030 年新廠投產再成長 +81%；（3）公
司積極切入新應用如 EMIB、四段式、玻璃基板、晶圓級壓模，最快
2027 年逐步落地。綜上所述，公司預期營收躍升將提前至 3Q26 發
生 ， 微幅上修 26 年營收從 30.1 億元提升至 30.7 億元（+62%YoY），
預期漲價、設備升級、產品組合有望帶動毛利率持續提升，預期毛利
率從 46.9% 提升至 47.4%，使得 26 年稅後 EPS 從 5.24 元上修
至 5.47 元。展望 27 年，公司擴產節奏提前，加上 27 年擴產幅度
上修 1-2 成，預期在產能滿載加上漲價效應下，27 年營收從 69.0 
億元上修至 88.9 億元 （+190%YoY） ， 毛利率從 52.9% 上修至 54.7%，
預期 27 年稅後 EPS 從 18.83 元上修到 24.19 元。考量公司具高
市占率領導地位，合約負債與擴產幅度不斷上修，同時積極發展多項
半導體新應用。公司 2H26 即將進入營收爆發期，長期成長趨勢陡峭
且明確，研究部給予 27 年 33 倍 PE，目標價從原先 600 元上修
到 800 元（2027 EPS*33xPER）。

重點摘要： 
1. 公司簡介 
長廣精機成立於 2022 年，由長興 （1717） 將電子材料事業單位
的電材設備專案組與 100% 持有日本子公司 Nikko Materials 
（NM） 切割而成立，同時引進非競爭同業萬潤、中鋼、群創開發
等策略投資人，公司持股高度集中於前十大投資人（72.9%），
其中又主要由母公司長興持有（61.7%）。公司提供真空壓膜機
設備產品之研發、製造與銷售，主要應用於 ABF 載板，在全球 
IC 載板產業市占率逾 95%，主要客戶包括欣興、南電、景碩、
三星電機、IBIDEN 等全球載版大廠。營運據點分別在總部日本
（高階設備生產） 、中國 （低階設備生產）與台灣（銷售與技術
支援） 。 主要競爭對手為志聖 、 群翊等 ， 不過公司為產業先行者 ，
過去和 ABF 材料廠龍頭味之素共同開發真空壓模技術發展多
年，且擁有日本 NM 的職人技術，使得公司製程品質最佳。公司
25 年真空壓模機設備營收佔比 94%，真空壓模機由低階到高階
可分為一段 、兩段和三段式設備 ， 三段壓模機又可分為 45 噸與 
90 噸兩種尺寸的機台。1H26 年產品組合為三段式 90 噸 28%、三
段式 45 噸 6%、二段式 27%、預貼機 27%、單段式 11%%。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 2Q26 產品組合轉佳速度提升，毛利率優於預期 
長廣 2Q26 營收 4.73 億元（+13.4%QoQ，+32.1%YoY），2Q26 認
列仍多以低階機台為主，加上受部分日幣匯率影響 1-2%。毛利
率 43.4%（+4.1ppts QoQ，-4.6ppts YoY），年減主因為產品組
合轉差，1H26 認列多以低階設備為主，不過優於原預期毛利率
41.4%，相較1Q26 產品組合持續優化。費用方面，1Q26 1.19 億
元，費用率28.5%，偏高主要來自IPO 與員工認股費用等。2Q26
則有所下降，費用 1.12 億元，費用率23.7%，優於原預期 1.33
億元，使得 2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.67 元，優於原先預期 0.47 元。

3. AI 晶片帶動 ABF 載板單位需求與整體需求倍數成長 
近年 ABF 高速擴張主要來自 AI 應用所驅動，AI 應用佔比將
於今明年突破三成。AI 用的高速運算 ABF 材料為了符合低電
阻等特性，樹脂含量較低，易產生氣泡與填充不均問題。長廣推
出 90 噸油壓式三段式真空壓膜機 ， 可更有效填充線路段差 、 控
制氣泡、降低溢膠，並改善大面積載板的平坦度與翹曲問題。因
此，AI 應用滲透率提升不僅帶動 ABF 載板需求增加，也推動
載板廠設備規格升級。預期 26 年起一線載板廠逾九成訂單為 
90 噸三段式設備，長期AI 晶片持續擴張有望使公司高階設備
佔比持續提升 ， 產品組合大幅轉佳帶動公司營收獲利 。 高階 ABF 
材料與大尺寸載板滲透率提升，長廣 90 噸三段式設備有望成
為公司營收與獲利成長的重要驅動。

長廣真空壓膜設備需求主要 受惠高階運算晶片出貨量成長
以及每顆晶片所需單位設備需求量提升。在 AI 基礎建設持
續擴張下，預期 24-28 年 AI GPU 與 ASIC 合計出貨量從 
1,330 萬顆成長至 3,050 萬顆，成長 2.29 倍（2024-2030 
年 CAGR+29%）。此外，AI 晶片世代升級帶動載板持續大型
化與多層化。

依 IBIDEN 技術 roadmap，25 年高階載板約 80×80mm、9x9 
層，26 年提升至 90×90mm、10x10 層，2030 年進一步達 
130×130mm、14x14 層， 單位需求量將成長逾 4 倍。綜上所
述，考量整體需求成長與單位需求成長的乘數效果 ，2030 年
整體載版設備需求量將成長近  9 倍（ 2025-2030 年 
CAGR+55%），產業需求與公司前景高成長且明確。

4. 合約負債與在手訂單增速與規模達歷史新高 
長廣合約負債主要來自客戶訂金，過往客戶在下單時將支付
1-3 成訂金，並在未來 3-4 季後認列。25 年底合約負債 
1.46 億元，當時公司依照客戶 Forecast 已見產業逐漸轉
為上行循環，推估在手訂單 7.3 億元。2Q26 財報公告合約
負債提升至 4.2 億元，近期公司預期在短短一個月後，合
約負債迅速提升至 8-9 億元，推估在手訂單從去年底 7.3 
億元提升 2Q26 21 億元再到目前 45 億元，在手訂單已達 
25 全年營收的 2.5 倍，將於未來一年逐漸兌現，顯示目前
載版廠對於真空壓模機設備需求又急又強，長期成長無虞。

5. 長廣持續受惠價量齊揚，積極提升擴產速度與幅度 
因應 AI 晶片龐大需求， 下游載板廠未來三年將大幅擴產 ， 而
高階真空壓膜機近乎只有長廣能供應， 市占率超過 95%。 面對
設備供不應求的現況加上材料成本上升 ， 公司受惠市場需求量
的成長與設備升級， 同時在今年四月將高階設備報價提升+10-
20%， 七月起再次將所有產品線報價提升+10-20%， 預期實際反
映於 4Q26。 
目前公司日本高階月產能與中國低階月產能分別約 18 台與 4
台，高階產能自 5M26 開始滿載，公司接單到認列約 3-4 季，
滿載後的營收規模將反應於 27 年。為因應龐大需求，短期公
司計劃在 2H26 逐步開出租賃廠房的產能與中國中低階產能。
原預期最晚到 27 年中分別達 22 台與 8 台，目前有望提前在 
4Q26 投產，10-12 月分別從 2-6 台月產能逐步開出，預期最
晚至 27 年中日本高階月產能有望達 28 台（+55%）。中國中
低階新產能則預期在 27 年農曆年後投產 （+100%） ，使得日本
得以專注生產高階設備，擠壓出更多高階設備產能。 
公司看好長期真空壓模機在各領域的需求發展 ， 同時也在日本
新建廠房，目前土地變更等建廠時程比預期緩慢，將從 28 年
延後至 29-30 年投產，不過年產能規劃將達 350 台，大幅優
於原先預期 60 台，作為日商背景，顯示長廣看見更長期的發
展趨勢，預期在目前規劃投產後的營收規模有望再成長近 3
倍。
